Of the types the log records, that is `installed_signage` and\n14: `temporary_banner` — a temporary banner is hung at the venue and is installed signage for\n15: every purpose in this standard.\n16: \n17: A `standalone_graphic` (artwork produced for downstream or personal use and never installed)\n18: and a `vehicle_wrap` (applied to a vehicle, not to the venue) are **not** installed signage.\n19: They are outside §2 to §4 whatever their own permit, clearance or size values say, and they\n20: do not sit against a zone's allowance under §5. Its `finding` entry holds every\n24: code it breaches, joined with `|`, in any order; an item that breaches none is `none`.\n25: \n26: The audit carries **one row per in-scope item**. An item §1 holds out of scope is not audited\n27: and does not appear in it — it is accounted for in the memo and in the figures instead.\n28: \n29: ## §2 Permit — `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`\n30: \n31: An installed-signage item must be covered by a festival permit that actually reaches it. A\n32: permit reaches an item only when the **governing** row of `permit_register.csv` satisfies all\n33: of:\n34: \n35: - it covers the item's zone (`covers_zones`, a `;`-joined list, or `all`);\n36: - it covers the item's recorded type (`covers_item_types`, likewise);\n37: - its `valid_until` is on or after the review date.\n38: \n39: The governing row is not always the row the item names. Where a permit has been re-issued,\n40: the register records the replacement with `supersedes` pointing at the row it replaces, and\n41: it is the **last** row in that chain whose terms apply. A chain may run more than one step,\n42: and a re-issue may be narrower than what it replaced — in zones, in item types, or in date.\n43: \n44: An item with no permit number, an unknown one, or one whose governing row fails any of the\n45: three tests is `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`.\n46: \n47: ## §3 Egress markers — `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED`\n48: \n49: Every zone sets its own minimum clearance from a designated safety egress marker, because the\n50: zones differ in crowd flow. What counts is the area of the items still standing\n67: — the in-scope items in that zone whose permits do reach them.\n68: \n69: A zone whose standing area exceeds its `aggregate_allowance_sqft` is `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`. A zone\n70: exactly on its allowance is within it. §5 is a property of the zone, not of any item in it,\n71: and is never a finding against an item.\n72: \n73: | zone | name | min clearance | per-item cap | zone allowance |\n74: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n75: | `ZN-01` | Main Gate Approach | 6.0 ft | 12.0 sq ft | 60.0 sq ft |\n76: | `ZN-02` | Riverside Promenade | 4.0 ft | 16.0 sq ft | 80.0 sq ft |\n77: | `ZN-03` | Craft Market Row | 4.0 ft | 8.0 sq ft | 40.0 sq ft |\n78: | `ZN-04` | Main Stage Apron | 8.0 ft | 10.0 sq ft | 50.0 sq ft |\n79: | `ZN-05` | Heritage Courtyard | 5.0 ft | 6.0 sq ft | 30.0 sq ft |\n80: | `ZN-06` | Food Court | 6.0 ft | 14.0 sq ft | 70.0 sq ft |\n81: | `ZN-07` | North Car Park | 3.0 ft | 20.0 sq ft | 90.0 sq ft |\n82: | `ZN-08` | Volunteer Compound | 3.0 ft | 10.0 sq ft | 45.0 sq ft |\n83: \n84: ## §6 Heightened inspection\n85: \n86: Where **3 or more** zones are in breach under §5, the festival is\n87: placed under heightened inspection.\n88: \n89: While heightened inspection is in force, the per-item size cap in §4 is reduced by\n90: **1 sq ft — in the breaching zones only**. It records the date the\n122: review is made as of; the logged items §1 holds out of scope, and why; every item carrying a\n123: finding, with the code and the record that decides it — the permit, the zone minimum, or the\n124: zone cap, named; any item whose numbers would read as a breach that the standard clears, and\n125: what clears it; the zones over their allowance, with the standing area and the allowance they\n126: passed; and whether heightened inspection is in force, with the count that put it there and\n127: what it changed.\n128: \n129: ## Finding names\n130: \n131: Item level: `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`, `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED`, `OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL`, or `none`.\n132: Zone level: `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`, or `none`.\n\n(End of file - total 132 lines)\n</content>","metadata":{"preview":"# Heritage Landing Festival — collateral and signage permit standard (HLF-7)\n\nRevision 7. Binding for every item recorded in `collateral_log.csv`. Where an item's own\nrecord and this standard disagree, this standard decides.\n\n**This review is made as of 2026-08-20.** Every currency test below is made\nagainst that date and nothing else. A permit whose validity ends **on**\n2026-08-20 is still current; one that ended before it is not.\n\n## §1 Scope — what counts as installed signage\n\n§2 to §4 apply to **installed signage**: collateral that is physically mounted or displayed\nat the venue. Of the types the log records, that is `installed_signage` and\n`temporary_banner` — a temporary banner is hung at the venue and is installed signage for\nevery purpose in this standard.\n\nA `standalone_graphic` (artwork produced for downstream or personal use and never installed)\nand a `vehicle_wrap` (applied to a vehicle, not to the venue) are **not** installed signage.\nThey are outside §2 to §4 whatever their own permit, clearance or size values say, and they\ndo not sit against a zone's allowance under §5. Reading the scope off a single literal type\nstring is the commonest error in this review.\n\nAn item carries one finding code for each rule it breaches. Its `finding` entry holds every\ncode it breaches, joined with `|`, in any order; an item that breaches none is `none`.\n\nThe audit carries **one row per in-scope item**. An item §1 holds out of scope is not audited\nand does not appear in it — it is accounted for in the memo and in the figures instead.\n\n## §2 Permit — `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`\n\nAn installed-signage item must be covered by a festival permit that actually reaches it. A\npermit reaches an item only when the **governing** row of `permit_register.csv` satisfies all\nof:\n\n- it covers the item's zone (`covers_zones`, a `;`-joined list, or `all`);\n- it covers the item's recorded type (`covers_item_types`, likewise);\n- its `valid_until` is on or after the review date.\n\nThe governing row is not always the row the item names. Where a permit has been re-issued,\nthe register records the replacement with `supersedes` pointing at the row it replaces, and\nit is the **last** row in that chain whose terms apply. A chain may run more than one step,\nand a re-issue may be narrower than what it replaced — in zones, in item types, or in date.\n\nAn item with no permit number, an unknown one, or one whose governing row fails any of the\nthree tests is `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`.\n\n## §3 Egress markers — `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED`\n\nEvery zone sets its own minimum clearance from a designated safety egress marker, because the\nzones differ in crowd flow. An item whose `egress_clearance_ft` is below its own zone's\n`min_egress_clearance_ft` is `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED`. Clearance exactly equal to the\nminimum meets it.\n\n## §4 Size — `OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL`\n\nEvery zone sets its own per-item size cap. An item whose `size_sqft` exceeds its own zone's\n`max_item_sqft` is `OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL`. A size exactly equal to the cap is within it.\n\n§6 can tighten this cap. Where it does, §4 is applied against the tightened figure.\n\n## §5 Zone allowance — `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`\n\nEach zone also carries a total allowance for the signage standing in it.\n\nAn item that fails §2 has no permit reaching it and comes down: it is **pulled**, and its\narea does not sit against the allowance. What counts is the area of the items still standing\n— the in-scope items in that zone whose permits do reach them.\n\nA zone whose standing area exceeds its `aggregate_allowance_sqft` is `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`. A zone\nexactly on its allowance is within it. §5 is a property of the zone, not of any item in it,\nand is never a finding against an item.\n\n| zone | name | min clearance | per-item cap | zone allowance |\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n| `ZN-01` | Main Gate Approach | 6.0 ft | 12.0 sq ft | 60.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-02` | Riverside Promenade | 4.0 ft | 16.0 sq ft | 80.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-03` | Craft Market Row | 4.0 ft | 8.0 sq ft | 40.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-04` | Main Stage Apron | 8.0 ft | 10.0 sq ft | 50.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-05` | Heritage Courtyard | 5.0 ft | 6.0 sq ft | 30.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-06` | Food Court | 6.0 ft | 14.0 sq ft | 70.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-07` | North Car Park | 3.0 ft | 20.0 sq ft | 90.0 sq ft |\n| `ZN-08` | Volunteer Compound | 3.0 ft | 10.0 sq ft | 45.0 sq ft |\n\n## §6 Heightened inspection\n\nWhere **3 or more** zones are in breach under §5, the festival is\nplaced under heightened inspection.\n\nWhile heightened inspection is in force, the per-item size cap in §4 is reduced by\n**1 sq ft — in the breaching zones only**. Every other zone keeps\nthe cap in the table above, and §2, §3 and §5 are unchanged.\n\nThe trigger reads the §5 breach count and nothing else. §5 counts standing area, and standing\ndepends on §2 alone, so §4 can never move the trigger: it is settled once the zone allowances\nare, and it does not move again.\n\n## §7 What the review reports\n\nA completed review closes with four records: the per-item verdicts, the per-zone rollup, a\nmemo to the safety office, and the batch figures.\n\n**The figures.** Eleven, every one taken over the review as HLF-7 settles it:\n\n| key | counts |\n| --- | --- |\n| `collateral_count` | in-scope items audited |\n| `zone_count` | venue zones |\n| `permit_missing_count` | in-scope items carrying `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING` |\n| `egress_obstructed_count` | in-scope items carrying `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED` |\n| `oversize_count` | in-scope items carrying `OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL` |\n| `compliant_count` | in-scope items carrying no finding |\n| `flagged_count` | in-scope items carrying at least one finding |\n| `finding_total` | findings raised across the batch |\n| `out_of_scope_count` | logged items §1 holds outside §2 to §4 |\n| `pulled_item_count` | items pulled under §5 |\n| `zones_over_allowance` | zones in breach under §5 |\n\n**The rollup.** One row per zone, carrying how many in-scope items it holds, how many were\npulled under §5, the standing area, the zone's allowance, and its §5 verdict.\n\n**The memo.** The safety office reads the memo, not the tables. It records the date the\nreview is made as of; the logged items §1 holds out of scope, and why; every item carrying a\nfinding, with the code and the record that decides it — the permit, the zone minimum, or the\nzone cap, named; any item whose numbers would read as a breach that the standard clears, and\nwhat clears it; the zones over their allowance, with the standing area and the allowance they\npassed; and whether heightened inspection is in force, with the count that put it there and\nwhat it changed.\n\n## Finding names\n\nItem level: `PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING`, `EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED`, `OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL`, or `none`.\nZone level: `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`, or `none`.","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":132,"totalLines":132,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/input/collateral_permit_standard.md","time":{"start":1787231310307,"end":1787231310484}},"id":"prt_01f4991cc001JJ8NopLJp4nXhw","sessionID":"ses_fe0b68dc5ffezUTgP8VPaZ3myT","messageID":"msg_01f498acb001XM7pLELV0y8QHK"}}

Actually, let me build a reverse map: supersedes_to -> list of permits that supersede it. Then follow.\n\n```python\nsupersedes_map = {}  # old_permit -> new_permit\nfor p, r in reg.items():\n    if r['supersedes'] and r['supersedes'] != 'none':\n        supersedes_map[r['supersedes']] = p  # assumes one-to-one\n```\n\nThen:\n```python\ndef governing(permit_no):\n    current = permit_no\n    seen = set()\n    while current in supersedes_map and current not in seen:\n        seen.add(current)\n        current = supersedes_map[current]\n    return current\n```\n\nThis follows the chain forward. For PMT-1004: supersedes_map[PMT-1004]=PMT-1005, supersedes_map[PMT-1005]=PMT-1006, supersedes_map[PMT-1006] not present → return PMT-1006. ✓\nFor PMT-1007: supersedes_map[PMT-1007]=PMT-1008, supersedes_map[PMT-1008] not present → return PMT-1008. ✓\n\nGood. Let me have the script build the memo content.\n\nLet me write the script to /tmp/opencode/audit.py.\n\nLet me draft the code:\n\n```python\nimport csv, json, os\nfrom collections import defaultdict, OrderedDict\n\nINPUT = '/app/input'\nOUT = '/app'\nREVIEW_DATE = '2026-08-20'\n\n# Load zones\nzones = {}\nwith open(os.path.join(INPUT, 'venue_zones.csv'), newline='') as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        zones[row['zone_id']] = {\n            'zone_name': row['zone_name'],\n            'min_egress_clearance_ft': float(row['min_egress_clearance_ft']),\n            'max_item_sqft': float(row['max_item_sqft']),\n            'aggregate_allowance_sqft': float(row['aggregate_allowance_sqft']),\n        }\n\n# Load permits\npermits = {}\nwith open(os.path.join(INPUT, 'permit_register.csv'), newline='') as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        permits[row['permit_no']] = {\n            'holder': row['holder'],\n            'covers_zones': row['covers_zones'],\n            'covers_item_types': row['covers_item_types'],\n            'valid_until': row['valid_until'],\n            'supersedes': row['supersedes'],\n        }\n\n# Build supersedes forward map\nsupersedes_map = {}\nfor p, r in permits.items():\n    s = r['supersedes']\n    if s and s != 'none':\n        supersedes_map[s] = p  # old -> new (assumes one replacement)\n\ndef governing(permit_no):\n    current = permit_no\n    seen = set()\n    while current in supersedes_map and current not in seen:\n        seen.add(current)\n        current = supersedes_map[current]\n    return current\n\ndef zone_covered(covers_zones, zone_id):\n    if covers_zones == 'all':\n        return True\n    return zone_id in covers_zones.split(';')\n\ndef type_covered(covers_types, item_type):\n    if covers_types == 'all':\n        return True\n    return item_type in covers_types.split(';')\n\n# Load items\nitems = []\nwith open(os.path.join(INPUT, 'collateral_log.csv'), newline='') as f:\n    for row in csv.DictReader(f):\n        items.append({\n            'item_id': row['item_id'],\n            'item_type': row['item_type'],\n            'zone_id': row['zone_id'],\n            'permit_no': row['permit_no'],\n            'egress_clearance_ft': float(row['egress_clearance_ft']),\n            'size_sqft': float(row['size_sqft']),\n            'installed_on': row['installed_on'],\n        })\n\nIN_SCOPE_TYPES = {'installed_signage', 'temporary_banner'}\n\n# Step 1: scope and §2 permit\nfor it in items:\n    it['in_scope'] = it['item_type'] in IN_SCOPE_TYPES\n    if it['in_scope']:\n        # §2\n        pn = it['permit_no']\n        if pn == 'none' or pn == '' or pn is None:\n            it['permit_ok'] = False\n            it['permit_reason'] = 'no permit number recorded'\n            it['gov_permit'] = None\n        elif pn not in permits:\n            it['permit_ok'] = False\n            it['permit_reason'] = f'permit {pn} not found in register'\n            it['gov_permit'] = None\n        else:\n            gov = governing(pn)\n            it['gov_permit'] = gov\n            gr = permits[gov]\n            zok = zone_covered(gr['covers_zones'], it['zone_id'])\n            tok = type_covered(gr['covers_item_types'], it['item_type'])\n            dok = gr['valid_until'] >= REVIEW_DATE\n            if zok and tok and dok:\n                it['permit_ok'] = True\n                it['permit_reason'] = None\n            else:\n                it['permit_ok'] = False\n                reasons = []\n                if not zok:\n                    reasons.append(f'governing {gov} covers zones {gr[\"covers_zones\"]}, not {it[\"zone_id\"]}')\n                if not tok:\n                    reasons.append(f'governing {gov} covers item types {gr[\"covers_item_types\"]}, not {it[\"item_type\"]}')\n                if not dok:\n                    reasons.append(f'governing {gov} valid_until {gr[\"valid_until\"]} (before {REVIEW_DATE})')\n                it['permit_reason'] = '; '.join(reasons)\n\n# Step 2: §5 standing area per zone and breach (using §2 only)\nstanding_area = defaultdict(float)\nin_scope_count = defaultdict(int)\npulled_count = defaultdict(int)\nfor it in items:\n    if not it['in_scope']:\n        continue\n    z = it['zone_id']\n    in_scope_count[z] += 1\n    if it['permit_ok']:\n        standing_area[z] += it['size_sqft']\n    else:\n        pulled_count[z] += 1\n\nzone_breach = {}\nfor z, zdata in zones.items():\n    zone_breach[z] = standing_area[z] > zdata['aggregate_allowance_sqft']\n\nbreach_zones = [z for z in zones if zone_breach[z]]\nheightened = len(breach_zones) >= 3\n\n# Step 3: effective cap per zone (§6 tightening in breach zones)\neff_cap = {}\nfor z, zdata in zones.items():\n    if heightened and zone_breach[z]:\n        eff_cap[z] = zdata['max_item_sqft'] - 1.0\n    else:\n        eff_cap[z] = zdata['max_item_sqft']\n\n# Step 4: §3 and §4 findings for in-scope items\nfor it in items:\n    if not it['in_scope']:\n        continue\n    z = it['zone_id']\n    zdata = zones[z]\n    findings = []\n    if not it['permit_ok']:\n        findings.append('PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING')\n    if it['egress_clearance_ft'] < zdata['min_egress_clearance_ft']:\n        findings.append('EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED')\n    if it['size_sqft'] > eff_cap[z]:\n        findings.append('OVERSIZE_COLLATERAL')\n    it['findings'] = findings\n\n# Build audit rows\naudit_rows = []\nfor it in items:\n    if it['in_scope']:\n        audit_rows.append((it['item_id'], '|'.join(it['findings']) if it['findings'] else 'none'))\n```\n\nThen compute figures, zone rollup, write files, build memo.\n\nLet me continue:\n\n```python\n# Figures\ncollateral_count = sum(1 for it in items if it['in_scope'])\nzone_count = len(zones)\npermit_missing_count = sum(1 for it in items if it['in_scope'] and 'PERMIT_NUMBER_MISSING' in it['findings'])\negress_obstructed_count = sum(1 for it in items if it['in_scope'] and 'EGRESS_MARKER_OBSTRUCTED' in it['findings'])\noversize_count = ('pulled_item_count', pulled_item_count),\n    ('zones_over_allowance', zones_over_allowance),\n])\n```\n\nWrite collateral_audit.csv:\n```python\nwith open(os.path.join(OUT, 'collateral_audit.csv'), 'allowance'])\n    for z in zones:  # preserve order\n        zdata = zones[z]\n        w.writerow([z, in_scope_count[z], pulled_count[z], f\"{standing_area[z]:.1f}\", Items failing §2 are pulled and their area does not count. An OVERSIZE or EGRESS item with a reaching permit still stands and still counts; only §2 pulls an item. A zone whose standing area exceeds its allowance is in breach. A zone exactly on its allowance is within it.\n117: \n118: | zone | name | in-scope items | pulled | standing area sqft | allowance sqft | verdict |\n119: | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\n120: | ZN-01 | Main Gate Approach | 7 | 3 | 45.5 | 60.0 | none |\n121: | ZN-02 | Riverside Promenade | 9 | 4 | 77.5 | 80.0 | none |\n122: | ZN-03 | Craft Market Row | 9 | 2 | 50.5 | 40.0 | ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH |\n123: | ZN-04 | Main Stage Apron | 6 | 1 | 48.0 | 50.0 | none |\n124: | ZN-05 | Heritage Courtyard | 7 | 3 | 23.5 | 30.0 | none |\n125: | ZN-06 | Food Court | 7 | 2 | 68.0 | 70.0 | none |\n126: | ZN-07 | North Car Park | 7 | 2 | 93.5 | 90.0 | ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH |\n127: | ZN-08 | Volunteer Compound | 8 | 3 | 48.0 | 45.0 | ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH |\n128: \n129: Zones in breach:\n130: \n131: - **ZN-03 (Craft Market Row)**: standing 50.5 sq ft > allowance 40.0 sq ft → `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`.\n132: - **ZN-07 (North Car Park)**: standing 93.5 sq ft > allowance 90.0 sq ft → `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`.\n133: - **ZN-08 (Volunteer Compound)**: standing 48.0 sq ft > allowance 45.0 sq ft → `ZONE_ALLOWANCE_BREACH`.\n134: \n135: ## Heightened inspection (§6)\n136: \n137: **Heightened inspection is in force.** The trigger is the §5 breach count: **3** zones are over their allowance (≥3), which placed the festival under heightened inspection. The trigger reads the §5 breach count and nothing else; it does not move again.\n138: \n139: What it changed: the per-item size cap in §4 is reduced by **1 sq ft — in the breaching zones only**. the row an item names.
